# 论文词库 1-1000

> 音标/释义来自 ECDICT；例句来自论文原文。先读例句，再记词。

- **however** /haʊˈɛvə/ — 然而（A · zk gk cet4 ky）｜“The fundamental constraint of sequential computation, however, remains.”
- **process** /pɹˈəʊsɛs/ — 处理（所有输入由同一神经网络处理）（S · gk cet4 cet6 ky toefl ielts）｜“31st Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NIPS 2017), Long Beach, CA, USA.”
- **scale** /skˈeɪl/ — 规模化，扩大（S · cet4 cet6 ky toefl ielts gre）｜“Noam proposed scaled dot-product attention, multi-head attention and the parameter-free position representation and became the other person involved in nearly every detail.”
- **evaluate** /ɪvˈaljuːˌeɪt/ — 评估（系统评测模型能力）（S · gk cet4 cet6 ky toefl ielts）｜“Jakob proposed replacing RNNs with self-attention and started the effort to evaluate this idea.”
- **improve** /ɪmpɹˈuːv/ — 改进，提升（S · zk gk cet4 cet6 ky）｜“Our model achieves 28.4 BLEU on the WMT 2014 Englishto-German translation task, improving over the existing best results, including ensembles, by over 2 BLEU.”
- **similar** /sˈɪmɪlə/ — a. 相似的, 类似的；n. 相似的东西（S · zk gk ielts）｜“Similar to the encoder, we employ residual connections around each of the sub-layers, followed by layer normalization.”
- **achieve** /ɐtʃˈiːv/ — 达到（成绩/效果）（S · zk gk cet4 cet6 ky toefl ielts）｜“Our model achieves 28.4 BLEU on the WMT 2014 Englishto-German translation task, improving over the existing best results, including ensembles, by over 2 BLEU.”
- **information** /ˌɪnfəmˈeɪʃən/ — n. 消息, 知识, 通知, 情报, 信息, 问讯处, 起诉；信息（S · zk gk ielts）｜“31st Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NIPS 2017), Long Beach, CA, USA.”
- **include** /ɪŋklˈuːd/ — vt. 包括, 把...算入, 包住；DOS内部命令:在CONFIG.SYS文件的一个配置块中包含另一配置块的内容（S · zk gk cet4 cet6 ky）｜“Gomez∗ † Łukasz Kaiser∗ Google Research University of Toronto Google Brain llion@google.com aidan@cs.toronto.edu lukaszkaiser@google.com Illia Polosukhin∗ ‡ illia.polosukhin@gmail.com Abstract The dominant sequence transduction models are based on complex recurrent or convolutional neural networks that include an encoder and a decoder.”
- **follow** /fˈɒləʊ/ — vt. 跟随, 沿行, 遵循, 追求；vi. 跟随, 接着（S · zk gk cet4 cet6 ky）｜“The Transformer follows this overall architecture using stacked self-attention and point-wise, fully connected layers for both the encoder and decoder, shown in the left and right halves of Figure 1, respectively.”
- **perform** /pəfˈɔːm/ — vt. 进行, 履行, 完成, 执行, 表演；vi. 行动, 工作, 执行, 演出（S · gk cet4 cet6 ky toefl）｜“The best performing models also connect the encoder and decoder through an attention mechanism.”
- **input** /ˈɪnpʊt/ — n. 输入, 输入电路；vt. 输入（S · cet4 cet6 ky）｜“Recurrent models typically factor computation along the symbol positions of the input and output sequences.”
- **representation** /ɹˌɛpɹɪzˈɛntˈeɪʃən/ — 表示（music representations 音乐的编码/表达形式，如音符、MIDI）（S · cet6 toefl）｜“Noam proposed scaled dot-product attention, multi-head attention and the parameter-free position representation and became the other person involved in nearly every detail.”
- **example** /ɛɡzˈampəl/ — n. 例子, 样本, 实例；实例（S · zk gk ielts）｜“This inherently sequential nature precludes parallelization within training examples, which becomes critical at longer sequence lengths, as memory constraints limit batching across examples.”
- **size** /sˈaɪz/ — n. 大小, 尺寸, 规模, 尺码, 能力, 浆料；vt. 上浆, 依大小排列（S · zk gk ielts）｜“Another way of describing this is as two convolutions with kernel size 1.”
- **compare** /kəmpˈeə/ — vt. 比较, 比喻, 对照；vi. 相比（S · zk gk cet4 cet6 ky toefl ielts）｜“4 Why Self-Attention In this section we compare various aspects of self-attention layers to the recurrent and convolutional layers commonly used for mapping one variable-length sequence of symbol representations (x1 , ..., xn ) to another sequence of equal length (z1 , ..., zn ), with xi , zi ∈ Rd , such as a hidden layer in a typical sequence transduction encoder or decoder.”
- **evaluation** /ɪvˌaljuːˈeɪʃən/ — 评估；检验模型性能的过程（A · cet6 gre）｜“Empirical evaluation of gated recurrent neural networks on sequence modeling.”
- **detail** /dˈiːteɪl/ — n. 细节, 详情；vt. 详述, 选派（S · cet4 cet6 ky ielts）｜“Noam proposed scaled dot-product attention, multi-head attention and the parameter-free position representation and became the other person involved in nearly every detail.”
- **generate** /dʒˈɛnəɹˌeɪt/ — 生成（S · cet4 cet6 ky toefl ielts gre）｜“Aligning the positions to steps in computation time, they generate a sequence of hidden states ht , as a function of the previous hidden state ht−1 and the input for position t.”
- **research** /ɹɪsˈɜːtʃ/ — n. 研究, 调查, 考察；vi. 研究, 调查（S · zk gk cet4 cet6 ky ielts）｜“Attention Is All You Need arXiv:1706.03762v7 \[cs.CL\] 2 Aug 2023 Ashish Vaswani∗ Noam Shazeer∗ Niki Parmar∗ Jakob Uszkoreit∗ Google Brain Google Brain Google Research Google Research avaswani@google.com noam@google.com nikip@google.com usz@google.com Llion Jones∗ Aidan N.”
- **apply** /ɐplˈaɪ/ — vt. 涂, 应用；vi. 申请, 适用（S · gk cet4 cet6 ky toefl ielts）｜“We show that the Transformer generalizes well to other tasks by applying it successfully to English constituency parsing both with large and limited training data. ∗ Equal contribution.”
- **sequence** /sˈiːkwəns/ — 序列（A · cet4 cet6 ky toefl ielts）｜“Gomez∗ † Łukasz Kaiser∗ Google Research University of Toronto Google Brain llion@google.com aidan@cs.toronto.edu lukaszkaiser@google.com Illia Polosukhin∗ ‡ illia.polosukhin@gmail.com Abstract The dominant sequence transduction models are based on complex recurrent or convolutional neural networks that include an encoder and a decoder.”
- **architecture** /ˈɑːkɪtˌɛktʃə/ — 架构（模型的整体结构设计）（S · gk cet4 cet6 ky toefl ielts）｜“We propose a new simple network architecture, the Transformer, based solely on attention mechanisms, dispensing with recurrence and convolutions entirely.”
- **parameter** /pəɹˈamɪtə/ — n. 参变数, 参变量, 参数, 参量；参量; 参数（S · cet6 ky toefl gre）｜“MultiHead(Q, K, V ) = Concat(head1 , ..., headh )W O where headi = Attention(QWiQ , KWiK , V WiV ) Where the projections are parameter matrices WiQ ∈ Rdmodel ×dk , WiK ∈ Rdmodel ×dk , WiV ∈ Rdmodel ×dv and W O ∈ Rhdv ×dmodel .”
- **following** /fˈɒləʊɪŋ/ — n. 下列各项, 部下, 追随者；a. 下列的, 其次的（S · gk cet4 cet6 ky）｜“In the following sections, we will describe the Transformer, motivate self-attention and discuss its advantages over models such as \[17, 18\] and \[9\].”
- **low** /lˈəʊ/ — n. 低点, 低价, 低, 牛叫声；a. 低的, 消沉的, 低等的, 浅的, 卑贱的（S · zk gk gre）｜“To summarize the research work conducted on instruction tuning LLaMA has become a major approach LLaMA, we present a brief evolutionary graph in Figure 5. to developing customized or specialized models, due to the relatively low computational costs.”
- **rate** /ɹˈeɪt/ — n. 比率, 率, 速度, 价格, 费用, 等级；vt. 估价, 认为, 鉴定等级, 责骂（S · gk cet4 cet6 ky ielts）｜“We varied the learning rate over the course of training, according to the formula: lrate = d−0.5 model · min(step_num −0.5 , step_num · warmup_steps−1.5 ) (3) This corresponds to increasing the learning rate linearly for the first warmup_steps training steps, and decreasing it thereafter proportionally to the inverse square root of the step number.”
- **step** /stˈɛp/ — n. 步骤, 步, 步幅, 脚步声, 踏级, 步伐, 短距离, 步态, 手段, 等级；vt. 踏, 以步测量, 跨步, 使成阶梯状（S · zk gk cet4 cet6 ky）｜“Aligning the positions to steps in computation time, they generate a sequence of hidden states ht , as a function of the previous hidden state ht−1 and the input for position t.”
- **increase** /ˈɪŋkɹiːs/ — n. 增加, 增进, 利益；vt. 增加, 加大（S · zk gk cet4 cet6 ky）｜“This would increase the maximum path length to O(n/r).”
- **note** /nˈəʊt/ — n. 笔记, 记录, 注解, 票据, 符号, 显要, 注重, 便笺, 照会；vt. 记录, 注解, 注意（S · zk gk toefl）｜“As noted in Table 1, a self-attention layer connects all positions with a constant number of sequentially executed operations, whereas a recurrent layer requires O(n) sequential operations.”
